---
description: Material the sample consists of.
icon: gem
---

# Material Type

Material type is a controlled list. Use it for the material the sample consists of — for example rock, mineral, water, or ice. Terms are grouped in a hierarchy; the web form shows the **full path**. Prefer the most specific term that fits.

**General material type** (`general_material_type`) is a broader term from this list. **Material types** (`material_types`) are more specific terms; a sample can have more than one.

Sample registration API writes expect the **leaf label** — the last part of the path. Batch upload accepts the full path or the leaf label.

If a term you need is missing, [request a term](request-a-term.md).

| Material type | for_registration | suppress_from_search |
| ------------- | ---------------- | -------------------- |

| Anthropogenic material > Concrete | true | false |
| Anthropogenic material > Manufactured chemical substance | true | false |
